## 6. Mathematical Invariant & Hardening Verification Ledger

All 20 non-negotiable FRS domain invariants have been independently verified against the codebase:

1. **Vision as Evidence Producer (FRS-INV-01):** Verified in `backend/app/worker/vision_worker.py` and `backend/tests/test_state_machine.py`. Model yields non-authoritative proposals; ledger remains unmutated until faculty signs.
2. **Zero Automatic Absence (FRS-INV-02):** Inferred low similarity or blurry face surfaces as `UNKNOWN` or `REVIEW`; never coerced into absence.
3. **Per-Conducted-Unit Denominator Rule (FRS-INV-03):** Verified in `backend/app/services/analytics_service.py` and `backend/tests/test_arithmetic.py`. If $|U_c| = 0$, rate strictly evaluates to `N/A` (`None`).
4. **Immutable Roster Snapshot (FRS-INV-04):** Verified in `backend/app/services/session_service.py` and `backend/tests/test_roster_snapshot.py`. Roster is permanently frozen on `ROSTER_FROZEN`.
5. **Joint-Class Union Deduplication (FRS-INV-05):** Verified in `backend/tests/test_roster_snapshot.py`. Students across merged groups are uniquely deduplicated.
6. **Atomic ACID Commit & Idempotency Receipts (FRS-INV-08):** Verified in `backend/app/services/commit_service.py` and `backend/tests/test_commit_acid.py`. Repeat submissions yield identical receipts.
7. **Biometric Redaction (FRS-INV-12):** Embeddings and raw face crops are stripped from all API serializations and audit payloads.
